That's way too slow.\\n\\n\\nActually, that speed through the proxy is really problematic — 15GB total would take roughly 40 hours. Let me try bypassing the network_turbo proxy entirely and connecting directly to Hugging Face instead, since the proxy might be the actual bottleneck here rather than helping. I should reconsider whether I really need different weights for the OFT model versus the standard base model, since I was assuming they'd diverge but maybe they don't.\\n\\nThe OFT paper fine-tunes from the pretrained OpenVLA-7b using LoRA, and the Hugging Face repo stores those base weights plus the unmerged LoRA adapter, so those base weights should match the original pretrained model exactly. But the model already on the server is the LIBERO fine-tuned version, not the pretrained one, which means the weights would actually be different. If I can locate the pretrained OpenVLA-7b model instead, that would solve the mismatch.\\n\\nAlternatively, I could load the pretrained base model, apply the LoRA adapter, then load the action head and proprio projector separately—but that still requires downloading the full 15GB pretrained model. if os.path.exists(out_path):\\n        existing_size = os.path.getsize(out_path)\\n    \\n    # Get total size\\n    try:\\n        head = requests.head(url, timeout=30, allow_redirects=True)\\n        total = int(head.headers.get('content-length', 0))\\n    except:\\n        total = 0\\n    \\n    if existing_size > 0 and existing_size == total:\\n        print(f\\\"[SKIP] {fname}: already headers=headers, stream=True, timeout=600, allow_redirects=True)\\n        if r.status_code not in (200, 206):\\n            print(f\\\"[FAIL] {fname}: HTTP {r.status_code}\\\", flush=True)\\n            return fname, False\\n        \\n        mode = 'ab' if existing_size > 0 and r.status_code == 206 else 'wb'\\n        downloaded = 0\\n        last_report = start\\n        with open(out_path, len(chunk)\\n                    now = time.time()\\n                    if now - last_report > 60:  # Report every 60s\\n                        speed = downloaded / (now - start) / 1024 / 1024\\n                        pct = (existing_size + downloaded) / total * 100 if total else 0\\n                        print(f\\\"[PROGRESS] {fname}: {pct:.1f}% ({speed:.1f} MB/s)\\\", flush=True)\\n small files first (sequential), then large files (2 parallel)\\nsmall_files = [f for f in FILES if 'model-0000' not in f]\\nlarge_files = [f for f in FILES if 'model-0000' in f]\\n\\nstart_all = time.time()\\nresults = {}\\n\\n# Small files first\\nfor f in small_files:\\n    name, ok = download_file(f)\\n    results[name] = ok\\n\\n# Large files with 2 parallel workers\\nwith ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:\\n    futures = {executor.submit(download_file, f): f for f in large_files}\\n    for future in as_completed(futures):\\n        name, ok =
